# JVMS 26 Coverage Ledger

This ledger is the required source of truth for implementing Java SE 26 JVM Specification coverage in visualize-jvm.

Status values:
- `PENDING`: not implemented yet.
- `IMPLEMENTED`: code exists and focused tests pass.
- `DIFFERENTIAL`: implementation also has HotSpot, `javap`, or ASM-oracle comparison where applicable.
- `N/A`: explicitly not a JVMS requirement for this project.

Every commit that implements a JVMS item must update the matching row with implementation and test references.

## Chapters 6 and 7 - Instruction Set and Opcode Mnemonics

Each opcode requires decoder coverage, verifier coverage when applicable, interpreter behavior or specified reserved-opcode error, and at least one focused test.

| Group | Opcodes | Module | Tests | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Opcode metadata | 0x00..0xff mnemonic, fixed-length, variable-length, and reserved opcode table | `jvm-interpreter` | `OpcodeMetadataTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Fixed-length decoder | Fixed-length non-reserved opcodes decode into offsets, metadata, and unsigned operands | `jvm-interpreter` | `BytecodeDecoderTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Tableswitch decoder | `tableswitch` 4-byte alignment, default/low/high header, and jump-offset table length | `jvm-interpreter` | `BytecodeDecoderTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Lookupswitch decoder | `lookupswitch` 4-byte alignment, default/npairs header, and match-offset pair table length | `jvm-interpreter` | `BytecodeDecoderTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Wide decoder | `wide` local-variable, `ret`, and `iinc` operand forms | `jvm-interpreter` | `BytecodeDecoderTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Constants | `nop`, `aconst_null`, `iconst_m1`, `iconst_0..5`, `lconst_0..1`, `fconst_0..2`, `dconst_0..1`, `bipush`, `sipush`, `ldc`, `ldc_w`, `ldc2_w` | `jvm-interpreter`, `jvm-verifier` | TBD | PENDING |
| Constants execution | `nop`, `aconst_null`, `iconst_m1..iconst_5`, `lconst_0..lconst_1`, `fconst_0..fconst_2`, `dconst_0..dconst_1`, `bipush`, and `sipush` mutate the runtime operand stack | `jvm-interpreter`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmInterpreterTest` | IMPLEMENTED |

| Native intrinsics, Phase 15 behavior suite | VM intrinsic registry resolves the committed Phase 15 native intrinsic surface as a single behavior guard | `jvm-interpreter` | `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.VM intrinsic registry*` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 2.5.5, 5.6 | Native library descriptors model logical library names, filesystem paths, JNI_OnLoad symbols, and exported guest method signatures with duplicate-export validation | `jvm-jni` | `JvmNativeLibraryDescriptorTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, descriptor binding | Native library catalog resolves configured library descriptors by logical `System.loadLibrary` name, rejects duplicate logical descriptors, and reports missing configured descriptors as native library load errors before lifecycle binding | `jvm-jni` | `JvmNativeLibraryCatalogTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, descriptor binding | Logical native library loader resolves `System.loadLibrary` logical names through the descriptor catalog before delegating to lifecycle loading and JNI_OnLoad registration, exposes `(String) -> Unit` load and unload hooks for interpreter native method contexts, and exposes symmetric logical unload through the same lifecycle boundary | `jvm-jni` | `JvmNativeLibraryLoaderTest.loadHook adapts loader into interpreter native library handler`, `JvmNativeLibraryLoaderTest.unloadHook adapts loader into interpreter native library handler`, `JvmNativeLibraryLoaderTest.unloadLibrary delegates logical unload through lifecycle` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, runtime entry | `java/lang/System.loadLibrary(Ljava/lang/String;)V` and `java/lang/Runtime.loadLibrary0(Ljava/lang/Class;Ljava/lang/String;)V` are available as VM intrinsics that extract guest `String` logical library names and delegate them to a VM-owned native-library load hook; full JDK `NativeLibraries` initialization wiring remains partial | `jvm-interpreter` | `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.System loadLibrary intrinsic delegates logical names to the native library load hook`, `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.Runtime loadLibrary0 intrinsic delegates logical names to the native library load hook` | PARTIAL |
| Native library loading, 5.6, runtime entry | Interpreter native dispatch threads VM-owned native-library load and unload hooks through top-level `invokestatic`, `invokevirtual`, `invokespecial`, and `invokeinterface` native method contexts, while `invokevirtual` also covers `Runtime.loadLibrary0`; static and instance native upcalls preserve the same lifecycle hooks when re-entering interpreted bytecode; dynamic-constant and `invokedynamic` bootstrap native methods can observe the unload hook; cached `invokedynamic` interpreted targets for virtual, special, interface, and constructor method handles retain the unload hook when nested bytecode re-enters native dispatch; `execute` can derive load/unload hooks from a configured `JvmNativeLibraryLoader` plus simulated `JavaVM`, so interpreted bytecode can request logical library lifecycle changes without leaving the simulated native boundary | `jvm-interpreter` | `JvmInterpreterTest.invokestatic System loadLibrary delegates to VM native library load hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokevirtual Runtime loadLibrary0 delegates to VM native library load hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokestatic System loadLibrary loads through configured native library loader`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokestatic NativeLibraries unload delegates to VM native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokevirtual native method context delegates to VM native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokespecial native methods retain native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokeinterface native methods retain native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.native static upcalls retain native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.native instance upcalls retain native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokedynamic native bootstrap retains native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.dynamic constant native bootstrap retains native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokedynamic cached linked virtual interpreted target retains native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokedynamic cached linked special interpreted target retains native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokedynamic cached linked interface interpreted target retains native library unload hook`, `JvmInterpreterTest.invokedynamic cached linked constructor interpreted target retains native library unload hook` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, runtime entry | `jdk/internal/loader/NativeLibraries.load(NativeLibraryImpl,String,boolean,boolean):boolean` is available as a VM intrinsic that validates the JDK native-library implementation object and boolean flags, delegates the guest `String` library name to the VM-owned native-library load hook, and returns JNI-style true on successful simulated load | `jvm-interpreter` | `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.NativeLibraries load intrinsic delegates library names to the native library load hook`, `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.NativeLibraries load intrinsic rejects non NativeLibraryImpl argument`, `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.NativeLibraries load intrinsic rejects invalid boolean flags` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, runtime entry | `jdk/internal/loader/NativeLibraries.findBuiltinLib(String):String` is available as a VM intrinsic that validates the guest `String` library name and returns guest null to keep builtin-library discovery inside the simulated boundary unless an explicit host/native binding is configured | `jvm-interpreter` | `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.NativeLibraries findBuiltinLib intrinsic reports no host builtin library` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, runtime entry | `jdk/internal/loader/NativeLibraries.unload(String,boolean,long):void` is available as a VM intrinsic that validates the guest library name, builtin flag, and native handle slot before delegating the name to the VM-owned native-library unload hook | `jvm-interpreter` | `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.NativeLibraries unload intrinsic delegates library names to the native library unload hook` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, runtime entry | `java/lang/System.mapLibraryName(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/String;` is available as a VM intrinsic that maps a guest logical library name through the host platform mapper and returns an interned guest `String` | `jvm-interpreter` | `JvmVmIntrinsicsTest.System mapLibraryName intrinsic returns the platform mapped library name` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, JNI binding | Native symbol name resolver emits JNI short and long names with class, method, and parameter descriptor mangling | `jvm-jni` | `JvmNativeSymbolNameResolverTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, JNI binding | Panama downcall backend skeleton resolves descriptor exports and raw symbols through an injected symbol lookup and reports unresolved exports as link errors | `jvm-jni` | `JvmPanamaDowncallBackendTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, JNI binding | JNI_OnLoad binding resolves optional library initialization symbols into downcall targets without failing absent libraries | `jvm-jni` | `JvmPanamaDowncallBackendTest.Panama backend binds optional JNI_OnLoad symbols` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, JNI binding | JNI_OnUnload binding resolves optional library finalization symbols into downcall targets without failing absent libraries | `jvm-jni` | `JvmPanamaDowncallBackendTest.Panama backend binds optional JNI_OnUnload symbols` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Native library loading, 5.6, JNI_OnLoad | `JNI_OnLoad` invocation frames pass a simulated `JavaVM` pointer and null reserved pointer to the optional library initializer without any guest method target | `jvm-jni` | `JvmPanamaDowncallBackendTest.Panama backend prepares JNI_OnLoad invocation with simulated JavaVM and reserved null` | IMPLEMENTED |

## Project-Specific Native and Host Coverage

| Area | Requirement | Module | Tests | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Host delegation | JDK and configured whitelist classes may execute as opaque host boundaries | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime`, `jvm-gui` | `JvmJdkHostDelegationTest`, `JvmWhitelistHostDelegationTest`, `HostDelegationEventsViewTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 5.3, 5.4, 6.5 | Default platform-class delegation is covered end-to-end through policy selection, host class mirroring, JDK static and instance method invocation, opaque boundary events, and static field reads | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmJdkHostDelegationTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 5.3, 5.4, 6.5 | Explicit class and package-prefix whitelist delegation covers non-platform host class mirroring plus static and instance host invocation while preserving explicit interpreted overrides | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmWhitelistHostDelegationTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 5.3, 5.4, 6.5 | Runtime class execution policy marks application classes interpreted by default, standard platform packages host-delegated by default, explicitly whitelisted classes/prefixes host-delegated, and explicit interpreted class names as overrides | `jvm-runtime` | `JvmClassExecutionPolicyTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 5.3, 5.4, 6.5 | Host delegated class mirrors bind a guest internal class name to the corresponding host `Class<?>`, expose the host binary name, mark the mirror as a host-delegated execution boundary, and reject mismatched guest/host names | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostDelegatedClassMirrorTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 5.3 | Isolated host class loader owns a host `URLClassLoader`, loads host classes by guest internal name without initialization, returns host-delegated class mirrors, and reports missing host classes with the original guest name | `jvm-host` | `JvmIsolatedHostClassLoaderTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 5.4, 6.5 | Host method resolver maps JVM method descriptors to host reflection parameter/return types, resolves public instance and static host methods, and rejects static/instance kind mismatches before invocation | `jvm-host` | `JvmHostMethodResolutionTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 5.4, 6.5 | Host field resolver maps JVM field descriptors to host reflection field types, resolves public instance and static host fields, and rejects static/instance kind and descriptor mismatches before access | `jvm-host` | `JvmHostFieldResolutionTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.3, 2.11.1, 5.4, 6.5 | Host primitive bridge converts guest primitive values to host reflection arguments and boxed primitive reflection returns back into guest primitive values while rejecting type mismatches and null primitive returns | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostPrimitiveBridgeTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 5.4, 6.5 | Host string bridge maps guest `java/lang/String` heap payloads and guest null references to host `String?`, and maps host `String?` returns back to VM-owned guest string or null references | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostStringBridgeTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 5.4, 6.5 | Host array bridge shallow-copies guest primitive array payloads and `String[]` reference arrays to host arrays, maps host primitive arrays and `String[]` returns back to VM-owned guest arrays, and preserves null array/string elements | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostArrayBridgeTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 5.3, 5.4, 6.5 | Host class mirror bridge maps guest `java/lang/Class` heap payloads to host `Class<?>` values and maps host `Class<?>` returns to interned VM-owned guest class mirror references while preserving null class values | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostClassMirrorBridgeTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 2.10, 5.4, 6.5 | Host throwable bridge maps guest throwable objects plus VM-owned stack-trace and cause payloads to host `Throwable` instances, and maps host throwable returns back to VM-owned guest throwable objects with stack-trace and cause payloads while preserving null throwable values | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostThrowableBridgeTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 5.4, 6.5 | Guest-host identity map maintains bidirectional object identity between guest object references and host objects using host reference identity rather than `equals`, and rejects conflicting rebindings | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostIdentityMapTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 5.4, 6.5 | Host static method invoker bridges guest arguments to host reflection values, invokes resolved public static host methods, maps primitive/reference/void returns back to guest values, and records opaque host object returns in the guest-host identity map | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostStaticMethodInvokerTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 5.4, 6.5 | Host instance method invoker bridges guest receivers and arguments to host reflection values, invokes resolved public instance host methods, maps primitive/reference/void returns back to guest values, and uses the guest-host identity map for opaque receivers | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostInstanceMethodInvokerTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 5.4, 6.5 | Host field accessor reads and writes resolved public static and instance host fields, bridges guest field values through primitive/reference conversions, and uses the guest-host identity map for opaque receivers and values | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostFieldAccessorTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Host delegation, 2.4, 2.7, 2.10, 5.4, 6.5 | Host exception translation converts `InvocationTargetException` targets thrown by delegated static and instance host methods into VM-owned guest throwable references with stack-trace payloads and exposes the translated throwable through a host boundary exception | `jvm-host`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmHostExceptionTranslationTest` | IMPLEMENTED |

| Simulated JNI | `SetObjectArrayRegion` writes nullable local handles into guest reference array ranges, accepts `jclass` handles as guest `java/lang/Class` mirror values, and rejects primitive arrays, invalid ranges, or non-assignable values | `jvm-jni`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.SetObjectArrayRegion*` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Simulated JNI | `Throw` records an existing guest throwable with observable pending-exception state, resolves `jclass` handles as guest `java/lang/Class` mirrors before rejecting them as non-`Throwable`, `ThrowNew` allocates a guest throwable and detail message, both reject non-`Throwable` inputs, `ExceptionOccurred` returns local handles without clearing it, `ExceptionDescribe` reports pending throwables without clearing them, `ExceptionCheck` reports pending state, `ExceptionClear` clears it, and `FatalError` raises a non-returning simulated fatal error | `jvm-jni`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.Throw records a pending guest throwable until ExceptionClear`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.ExceptionOccurred returns local handles without clearing the pending exception`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.ThrowNew allocates a guest throwable with a detail message and records it pending`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.Throw rejects non throwable guest object handles`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.Throw rejects jclass handles as non throwable guest Class mirror objects`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.ThrowNew rejects non throwable guest class handles`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.ExceptionDescribe*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.FatalError*` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Simulated JNI | `GetJavaVM` returns the owning simulated `JavaVM` for VM-bound simulated `JNIEnv` instances through both the environment API and function table, and reports JNI error for unbound standalone environments | `jvm-jni` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.GetJavaVM*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.JNIEnv function table delegates GetJavaVM*`, `JvmSimulatedJniFunctionTableMetadataTest` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Simulated JNI | `NewLocalRef` duplicates object and class local reference handles and preserves `null`; `IsSameObject` compares nullable object and class reference handles by guest identity, including `jclass` handles against ordinary `jobject` handles for the same guest `java/lang/Class` mirror; `DeleteLocalRef` deletes object local handles and ignores `null`; `EnsureLocalCapacity` records the requested guaranteed local-reference capacity, keeps the maximum requested capacity, returns JNI success, and rejects negative capacities; `PushLocalFrame` records nested local-frame depth, maximum requested frame capacity, and enforces the current frame capacity when allocating local references while rejecting non-positive capacities; `PopLocalFrame` pops local-frame depth, deletes handles allocated in the popped frame, returns `null` for null results, rebinds non-null object and class-reference results as fresh local handles in the previous frame, and rejects underflow | `jvm-jni` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.NewLocalRef*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.IsSameObject*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.DeleteLocalRef*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.EnsureLocalCapacity*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.PushLocalFrame*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.PopLocalFrame*` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Simulated JNI | `NewGlobalRef` creates nullable object and class reference handles outside local-frame ownership; `NewWeakGlobalRef` creates nullable object and class weak-global handles outside local-frame ownership; global and weak-global handles survive `PopLocalFrame`; `DeleteGlobalRef` and `DeleteWeakGlobalRef` release only their own reference scopes while ignoring `null`; local/global/weak-global delete helpers reject handles from the wrong reference scope without deleting them; `GetObjectRefType` reports local, global, weak-global, invalid/null, and non-reference `jmethodID`/`jfieldID` handles | `jvm-jni` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.NewGlobalRef*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.NewWeakGlobalRef*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.DeleteLocalRef and DeleteGlobalRef*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.DeleteWeakGlobalRef*`, `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.GetObjectRefType*` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Simulated JNI | `MonitorEnter` resolves guest object handles, including `jclass` handles as guest `java/lang/Class` mirror objects, and records reentrant ownership in guest monitor state for the current simulated JNI thread | `jvm-jni`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.MonitorEnter*` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Simulated JNI | `MonitorExit` resolves guest object handles, including `jclass` handles as guest `java/lang/Class` mirror objects, and decrements or releases ownership in guest monitor state for the current simulated JNI thread | `jvm-jni`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.MonitorExit*` | IMPLEMENTED |
| Simulated JNI | JNI refs, strings, arrays, fields, and monitors mutate one shared guest state across helper families | `jvm-jni`, `jvm-runtime` | `JvmSimulatedJniEnvironmentTest.JNI data helpers mutate one guest state for refs strings arrays fields and monitors` | IMPLEMENTED |

The project is not complete until every non-`N/A` row is at least `IMPLEMENTED`, and every row with an external comparison surface is `DIFFERENTIAL`.
